## [0.8.3](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.8.3)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлена интеграция OpenHands со встроенной настройкой MCP и правилами извлечения и сохранения памяти.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/65862c4fe" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>65862c4fe</a>
- Добавлена интеграция с редактором Zed через сервер контекста MCP с правилами извлечения и сохранения памяти.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/539101af3" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>539101af3</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Continue.dev через HTTP-поставщик контекста.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c05ab9103" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c05ab9103</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Composio: память Hindsight можно применять как свои инструменты.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/1c9ba0e65" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>1c9ba0e65</a>
- Добавлено приложение Zapier с действиями и триггерами REST-хуков для автоматизации работы с Hindsight.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/359b2bc76" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>359b2bc76</a>
- Добавлена конечная точка API для пробного извлечения фактов: результат можно посмотреть без сохранения.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d382b340f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d382b340f</a>
- Добавлена необязательная поддержка OCR на базе MarkItDown для извлечения текста из документов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Sanderhoff-alt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Sanderhoff-alt</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/5ee53c512" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>5ee53c512</a>
- Улучшено понимание запросов о времени на китайском языке для более точного поиска.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Sanderhoff-alt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Sanderhoff-alt</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d0c54560a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d0c54560a</a>
- Для инструментов MCP добавлены пометки о чтении и опасных действиях, чтобы клиент работал безопаснее.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/yunanwg" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@yunanwg</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/27aa6bbf4" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>27aa6bbf4</a>
- Область наблюдений "shared" теперь поддерживается при консолидации и в интерфейсе Control Plane.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0a046f97a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0a046f97a</a>
- Точное совпадение тегов (tags_match=exact) теперь поддерживается в интерфейсе и клиентских библиотеках.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/2f075dede" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>2f075dede</a>
- Добавлено обнаружение Intel XPU для ускорения локального создания векторов, если устройство доступно.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/2fb2de1aa" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>2fb2de1aa</a>

**Улучшения**

- Добавлены метрики очереди операций и рабочих процессов для контроля асинхронной обработки.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/MattJackson" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@MattJackson</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b1fe23fbe" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b1fe23fbe</a>
- Добавлены настройки уровня обслуживания Gemini.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d68bd0742" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d68bd0742</a>
- Ответы API теперь опускают поля со значением null, когда это безопасно для формата передачи, чтобы уменьшить объём данных.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/e20a36c95" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>e20a36c95</a>
- Улучшена оценка свежести результатов поиска: если нужно, берётся время действия записи.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/3d6b19af5" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>3d6b19af5</a>
- Миграции схем для нескольких арендаторов теперь идут параллельно и занимают меньше времени.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/460fc63d9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>460fc63d9</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Исправлено разбиение больших документов при сохранении: текст больше не теряется при делении на несколько партий.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/81865bf87" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>81865bf87</a>
- Исправлен MCP: свои инструкции для инструментов снова применяются верно.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Sanderhoff-alt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Sanderhoff-alt</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f4bac2d41" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f4bac2d41</a>
- Ответы MCP по умолчанию больше не содержат трассировки инструмента reflect и языковой модели.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/yunanwg" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@yunanwg</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/44972d321" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>44972d321</a>
- Для вызовов LiteLLM задано жёсткое время ожидания, чтобы зависший вызов не блокировал работу навсегда.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/kubaodias" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@kubaodias</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/551932991" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>551932991</a>
- В списке банков теперь единообразно применяются настройки характера банка и наложения задач.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/PathKnower" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@PathKnower</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/cb73790c2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>cb73790c2</a>
- Пробное извлечение теперь отклоняет пустой текст и запускается лишь после начальной проверки.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a92e25bcf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a92e25bcf</a>
- Консолидация больше не срывается, если языковая модель возвращает одно значение source_fact_ids.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/haodonp" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@haodonp</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/94619ce52" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>94619ce52</a>
- Исправлены метки метрик для HTTP-точек: ID банков подставляются по шаблону, чтобы число меток не росло без нужды.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/sireika" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@sireika</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4efa20472" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4efa20472</a>
- После сброса квоты поставщика повторы запросов откладываются, чтобы не создавать лавину вызовов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/oldschoola" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@oldschoola</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/2b521c3a0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>2b521c3a0</a>
- Для поиска Codex auth.json теперь учитывается CODEX_HOME; вход работает и при своём домашнем каталоге.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/mdenil" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@mdenil</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0c9bc765c" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0c9bc765c</a>
- В интерфейсе добавления документа Control Plane теперь можно выбрать область наблюдений “shared”.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0ca0226d3" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0ca0226d3</a>
- Из неструктурированных ответов теперь удаляются неверно оформленные теги и блоки рассуждений.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/grimmjoww" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@grimmjoww</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/7b17da7a0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>7b17da7a0</a>

## [0.8.2](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.8.2)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлена поддержка областей наблюдений: списки, фильтрация, показ и лимиты наблюдений для каждой области.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b08f43496" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b08f43496</a>
- В события вебхука Memory Defense добавлены поля для SIEM, чтобы оповещения безопасности содержали больше контекста.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/chrislatimer" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@chrislatimer</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f304ce7e0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f304ce7e0</a>
- Добавлена обратимая правка памяти: её элементы можно менять, помечать недействительными и возвращать к прежнему виду.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/de22b606e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>de22b606e</a>
- Расходы на поставщика теперь можно учитывать по банкам через поле OpenAI "user".<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/sireika" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@sireika</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/fb16fc4fd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>fb16fc4fd</a>
- Добавлен встроенный поставщик языковых моделей Nous Portal со входом через OAuth без внешней командной строки.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/82c7df726" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>82c7df726</a>
- Добавлена проверка связи с языковой моделью для каждого банка, чтобы проверять настройки поставщика.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0d55a9b78" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0d55a9b78</a>
- Для извлечения фактов при сохранении добавлена поддержка Gemini Batch API.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a942b1c81" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a942b1c81</a>
- Добавлена поддержка семейства Gemini embedding-2 с векторами для каждого входного текста.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/22ae72a90" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>22ae72a90</a>
- При загрузке памяти файлы JSONL теперь делятся по строкам, чтобы записи не разрывались.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4e7780e59" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4e7780e59</a>
- В интерфейсе добавлен экспорт созвездия в SVG-плакат, которым можно поделиться.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0280b3486" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0280b3486</a>
- Добавлен плагин Cursor с долговременной памятью Hindsight.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/91d767cdc" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>91d767cdc</a>
- Добавлен пакет интеграции Cline для установки через pip (hindsight-cline).<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/36e31c675" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>36e31c675</a>
- Добавлен пакет интеграции Cursor CLI для установки через pip (hindsight-cursor-cli).<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c6dd08944" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c6dd08944</a>
- Добавлена интеграция памяти Hindsight с Microsoft Agent Framework без MCP.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f5a6c300f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f5a6c300f</a>

**Улучшения**

- Для встроенных установок добавлено локальное веб-приложение Control Center.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4032b2791" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4032b2791</a>
- Синтез ответа reflect теперь точнее соблюдает указания и правила языка.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/90ee101be" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>90ee101be</a>
- Добавлен выбор уровня обслуживания Amazon Bedrock.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/mobilinkd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@mobilinkd</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/27cb1c684" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>27cb1c684</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Экран банка в Control Plane больше не загружает граф дважды, что ускоряет работу и убирает лишние запросы.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d81486ff9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d81486ff9</a>
- Лимит наблюдений по области теперь работает верно: значение 0 запрещает создавать новые наблюдения.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f6710963e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f6710963e</a>
- При разрыве связи оставшиеся операции recall и reflect теперь надёжно отменяются.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/07c85da98" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>07c85da98</a>
- Исправлена гонка: запуск модели больше не меняет глобальный тип данных Torch по умолчанию, поэтому float32 ведёт себя одинаково.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/30fb287d1" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>30fb287d1</a>
- В данных вебхуков добавлены поля, которые прежде пропускались.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/chrislatimer" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@chrislatimer</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/87448b161" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>87448b161</a>
- Для хранения истории в PostgreSQL поле bank_id переведено в TEXT, чтобы длинные ID не вызывали сбой.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/621ab7e66" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>621ab7e66</a>
- Командная строка теперь верно разбирает ответы get-memory.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/72d9881a6" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>72d9881a6</a>
- При создании заметок встречи Control Plane теперь убирает пустые сообщения, на которых могла сбоить проверка локали.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/8e6dc5fcd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>8e6dc5fcd</a>
- При поэтапном развёртывании пропуск миграций теперь надёжнее обрабатывает вложенные ошибки поиска зависимостей.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/bionicbutterfly13" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@bionicbutterfly13</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0afa046fc" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0afa046fc</a>

## [0.8.1](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.8.1)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлен флаг API, который отключает хранение исходного текста документа.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/1296e9fc1" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>1296e9fc1</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Oh-My-OpenAgent (OMO) с памятью Hindsight.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/dcbouius" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@dcbouius</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/6dc56498c" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>6dc56498c</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Cline: хуки событий сохраняют и извлекают память Hindsight.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/66e58a23a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>66e58a23a</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Haystack, где Hindsight служит памятью для конвейеров.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/394d66e60" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>394d66e60</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Cursor CLI для записи и применения памяти Hindsight в процессах Cursor.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Korayem" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Korayem</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/dbfe83a2a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>dbfe83a2a</a>

**Улучшения**

- Добавлен официальный плагин Obsidian для синхронизации и применения памяти Hindsight.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b0f86f9c0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b0f86f9c0</a>
- Интеграция Roo Code выпущена как пакет PyPI с командной строкой (hindsight-roo-code).<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/644e37ac1" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>644e37ac1</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Настройка vchordrq.probes больше не навязывается индексам vchord, которые её не поддерживают.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/109e1bd95" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>109e1bd95</a>
- Служебные процедуры базы данных теперь верно ставятся и при схеме public.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/95d77233b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>95d77233b</a>

## [0.8.0](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.8.0)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлено фоновое обслуживание по расписанию: оно согласует состояние консолидации и соблюдает сроки хранения у всех арендаторов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/e77461762" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>e77461762</a>
- Для долгой консолидации и пакетного сохранения памяти добавлены устойчивые снимки прогресса: состояние можно верно посмотреть и продолжить.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c94935bfa" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c94935bfa</a>
- Трассировка запросов к языковой модели включена по умолчанию, в том числе по банкам; записи хранятся сутки для отладки.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f4a3329ce" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f4a3329ce</a>
- При консолидации добавлено смысловое удаление почти одинаковых наблюдений. Оно включено по умолчанию, кроме Oracle.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/8aa31edd4" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>8aa31edd4</a>
- История ментальных моделей и наблюдений вынесена в отдельные таблицы базы данных для надёжной работы при росте объёма.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/7e1145c08" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>7e1145c08</a>
- Добавлен локальный поставщик векторов ONNX: векторы можно создавать без внешнего API.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/shoveller" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@shoveller</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b5a324b77" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b5a324b77</a>
- Добавлены экспорт и импорт целого банка для переноса между экземплярами.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/602c9f55e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>602c9f55e</a>
- Документы можно переносить между банками без нового вызова языковой модели, используя уже извлечённые данные.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/1d6d73bce" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>1d6d73bce</a>
- Добавлен общий для всех поставщиков кэш начала запроса. Он включён по умолчанию и снижает число повторных вызовов модели при сохранении, консолидации и reflect.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/cdbartholomew" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@cdbartholomew</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d7a3aa526" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d7a3aa526</a>
- Через HINDSIGHT_API_LLM_EXTRA_BODY теперь можно добавлять поля в запросы к любому поставщику.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/01296d8d5" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>01296d8d5</a>
- Параметр HINDSIGHT_API_LLM_STRICT_SCHEMA теперь действует у всех поставщиков моделей с поддержкой JSON Schema для строгого формата ответов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a3d3d42b3" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a3d3d42b3</a>
- Добавлена интеграция защитного промежуточного слоя Superagent для более безопасной работы агентов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b70830218" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b70830218</a>

**Улучшения**

- Обёртки Python и TypeScript теперь показывают в трассировке детали вызовов инструмента reflect и языковой модели.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0db70bb88" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0db70bb88</a>
- Через переменные среды можно задать усиление поиска для каждой стратегии извлечения памяти.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/01134047d" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>01134047d</a>
- Чувствительность смыслового поиска можно менять через порог сходства HINDSIGHT_API_SEMANTIC_MIN_SIMILARITY.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/zwcf5200" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@zwcf5200</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/aa024a5cd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>aa024a5cd</a>
- Если свежая ментальная модель подходит для reflect, лишний вызов языковой модели больше не нужен.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c255d3552" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c255d3552</a>
- Запросы статистики банка стали быстрее и легче за счёт кэша и более точного расчёта.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/cdbartholomew" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@cdbartholomew</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/7683f2900" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>7683f2900</a>
- Если аудит или запросы к языковой модели отключены, интерфейс Control Plane ясно показывает это.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/283419280" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>283419280</a>
- В интеграции OpenCode улучшены журналы отладки, показ конечной точки и ошибок.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/796a9eff9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>796a9eff9</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Для консолидации задан явный лимит токенов ответа, чтобы операции не срывались и не обрезали результат.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xmh1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xmh1011</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c2524473e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c2524473e</a>
- Поиск по времени стал быстрее и дешевле: число просматриваемых записей ограничено для каждого типа фактов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4c33a4e55" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4c33a4e55</a>
- Исправлены сбои поиска pgroonga BM25 за счёт верного экранирования текста запроса.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xmh1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xmh1011</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/6b8fc53d7" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>6b8fc53d7</a>
- В трассировках и результатах поиска сохранены верные исходные ранги RRF.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/zwcf5200" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@zwcf5200</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f62500193" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f62500193</a>
- API больше не висит при запуске: если инициализация модели блокируется, ошибка выдаётся сразу.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4f5003480" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4f5003480</a>
- Исправлена работа истории извлечения памяти и ментальных моделей с базой Oracle.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/23710f4a8" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>23710f4a8</a>
- Перед записью в pgvector проверяется размерность векторов, чтобы избежать ошибок хранения и поиска.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/ai-ag2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@ai-ag2026</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/06c88e043" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>06c88e043</a>
- Перед извлечением фактов повторно проверяется свежесть; одновременная запись в один документ идёт по очереди.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/23168ebf6" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>23168ebf6</a>
- Исправлена неверная привязка фактов при сохранении из-за утечки ключа маршрутизации банка.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d695611ad" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d695611ad</a>
- Перед записью ментальной модели проверяется наличие банка, чтобы её создание не срывалось.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/56b4271d9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>56b4271d9</a>
- Для внешнего Postgres с vchord нужные каталоги добавлены в search_path сеанса.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/049901802" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>049901802</a>
- Отдельный запуск в Docker теперь ясно сообщает об ошибке прав на bind-mount pg0.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/75a7c19d6" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>75a7c19d6</a>
- Если сервер модели неверно обрабатывает tool_choice="required", система переходит на более совместимый режим выбора инструмента.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/e30f8af14" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>e30f8af14</a>
- Ключ API переранжирования стал необязательным, чтобы вход через Bedrock IAM работал с LiteLLM.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/3c8ca47dd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>3c8ca47dd</a>
- Добавлены метаданные итога сохранения памяти, чтобы было видно, что произошло.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xmh1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xmh1011</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/831f0efa1" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>831f0efa1</a>
- Изменения настроек банка через PATCH теперь сохраняются и для банков, где ещё нет данных.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a809547aa" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a809547aa</a>
- Из запроса для извлечения фактов убраны маркеры жирного текста Markdown, которые мешали извлечению.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Oxygen56" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Oxygen56</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/50b7eda2a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>50b7eda2a</a>
- Поиск памяти стал устойчивее и быстрее: VectorChord BM25 применяется при нужном условии, а число кандидатов ограничено для каждого источника.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/70d98c7a2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>70d98c7a2</a>
- Исправлен перевод операций и подписей графа в интерфейсе Control Plane.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/MapleEve" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@MapleEve</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a14ce623c" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a14ce623c</a>
- Сводки импорта в командной строке теперь показывают число записей mental_model_history.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/3346363d2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>3346363d2</a>

## [0.7.2](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.7.2)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлена интеграция памяти Hindsight с агентами Google ADK.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c032a74f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c032a74f</a>
