- Добавлена интеграция Flowise с инструментами памяти Hindsight.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/41ad2b55" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>41ad2b55</a>
- Добавлен Fireworks AI как поставщик пакетного вывода.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Fyko" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Fyko</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/537b2812" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>537b2812</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Roo Code с памятью Hindsight через MCP, включая поддержку правил.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/bf6b9026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>bf6b9026</a>
- Добавлена интеграция через вебхук Vapi для записи разговоров голосового агента в память Hindsight.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/dde133da" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>dde133da</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Gemini Spark через MCP.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/7cdacc4b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>7cdacc4b</a>

**Улучшения**

- Ускорено обслуживание графа при подготовке векторного поиска и индекса ANN.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/99c7367f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>99c7367f</a>
- Для ANN в vchord взят косинусный класс операций и настройки по типу сервера, чтобы векторный поиск работал вернее.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/isac322" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@isac322</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/e4686b92" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>e4686b92</a>
- Сокращена пауза между повторами консолидации для быстрого выхода из временных сбоев.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f49b85c0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f49b85c0</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Закреплена версия Next.js, чтобы убрать цикл переадресации из-за языковых настроек Control Plane.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/201f5d7c" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>201f5d7c</a>
- Число потоков машинного обучения ограничено доступными ядрами CPU, чтобы снизить расход ресурсов и риск сбоев.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0a2ee845" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0a2ee845</a>
- Исправлены ошибки обновления PostgreSQL в установках v0.7.x.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b7f267b0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b7f267b0</a>
- В резервную копию и восстановление добавлены ранее пропущенные таблицы.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/867b7b4a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>867b7b4a</a>
- При параллельной работе консолидация памяти стала надёжнее за счёт блокировок и отправки по областям и атомарной постановки задач.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/08ce8176" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>08ce8176</a>
- Исправлено сохранение больших документов: индексы частей верны, полный текст не теряется.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/364ccf17" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>364ccf17</a>
- Сохранение и извлечение памяти лучше обрабатывают редкие символы Unicode, включая буквальные спецтокены и одиночные суррогаты.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4bc7013e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4bc7013e</a>
- Исправлена гонка при добавлении связей памяти во время сохранения.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/8cf0dcbf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>8cf0dcbf</a>
- Запуск встроенной фоновой службы в Windows больше не открывает лишнюю вкладку терминала.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4280ac3f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4280ac3f</a>
- Обновлена зависимость встроенной базы данных для надёжной остановки и перезапуска Postgres.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/8d9000a8" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>8d9000a8</a>
- Исправлена обработка времени сохранения памяти в командной строке и список допустимых типов фактов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/slayoffer" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@slayoffer</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/df73c792" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>df73c792</a>
- Список директив и reflect теперь учитывают группы тегов при фильтрации.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/fb554664" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>fb554664</a>
- Фоновая служба теперь учитывает явно заданные адрес и порт.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Sanderhoff-alt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Sanderhoff-alt</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/77380211" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>77380211</a>
- Дочерний процесс поставщика Claude Code отделён от пользовательских плагинов, чтобы они не мешали его работе.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/bcae23d9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>bcae23d9</a>
- Перед возвратом порта фоновая служба ждёт успешной проверки состояния, что повышает надёжность запуска.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/s09x" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@s09x</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/5e547f71" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>5e547f71</a>
- Повторы задач теперь соблюдают заданный предел попыток.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/aaronwestphal" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@aaronwestphal</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/85f6769e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>85f6769e</a>
- При ошибке извлечения фактов сохранение памяти больше не пропадает без сообщения.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/6e734e1a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>6e734e1a</a>
- Переадресация после входа в Control Plane теперь проверяет адрес возврата и учитывает basePath, что повышает безопасность и точность маршрута.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/9571a341" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>9571a341</a>
- Для поставщиков, которые уже возвращают оценки в диапазоне [0,1], исходные оценки переранжирования сохраняются.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/18b9c596" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>18b9c596</a>

## [0.7.1](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.7.1)

**Новые возможности**

- Усилие рассуждения языковой модели можно задать через переменную среды.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/s9rkn" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@s9rkn</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/1890d2b7" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>1890d2b7</a>
- Добавлены переменные среды для времени ожидания HTTP-запросов переранжирования по поставщикам.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a510b07a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a510b07a</a>
- В интерфейсе Control Plane добавлены варианты китайского языка.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/MapleEve" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@MapleEve</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/617939d8" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>617939d8</a>

**Улучшения**

- Задачи консолидации теперь идут по приоритетам банков: более важные обрабатываются первыми.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/cf637799" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>cf637799</a>
- В фильтре типов операций Control Plane добавлено значение "graph_maintenance".<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/691cb539" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>691cb539</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Консолидация стала надёжнее: попытки повторяются с паузой без предела, а одинаковые задачи для банка не дублируются.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/0d2ba56f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>0d2ba56f</a>
- Исправлена порча памяти при сохранении больших документов, не вмещавшихся в один вызов модели: часть извлечённых сведений могла пропасть. После обновления сохраните затронутые документы снова.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/74525cc0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>74525cc0</a>
- Добавлено обновление токена и сопутствующие правки для устойчивой работы векторов Codex OAuth.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/ffa6fbf2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>ffa6fbf2</a>
- Оценка свежести памяти теперь привязана ко времени запроса для более ровных результатов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Sanderhoff-alt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Sanderhoff-alt</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/2de19e57" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>2de19e57</a>

## [0.7.0](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.7.0)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлена поддержка ParadeDB pg_search как сервера поиска BM25, совместимого с Citus.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4cd260b6" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4cd260b6</a>
- Добавлены настройки токенизации pg_search для точного управления разбиением текста при поиске.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Sanderhoff-alt" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Sanderhoff-alt</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/16f80769" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>16f80769</a>
- Добавлен новый поставщик векторов ZeroEntropy.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/flamerged" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@flamerged</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/ec49175f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>ec49175f</a>
- Добавлен вход через Codex OAuth для создания векторов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/haha0815" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@haha0815</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d802f914" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d802f914</a>
- Добавлена конечная точка API для очистки текста ментальной модели.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/7bd11bed" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>7bd11bed</a>
- Консолидацию можно ограничить областью наблюдений и вести только в нужной части памяти.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/ac3ab2b5" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>ac3ab2b5</a>
- Добавлена поддержка модели переранжирования Alibaba Qwen3.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/quicklyfast" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@quicklyfast</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b83bb87d" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b83bb87d</a>
- Добавлена интеграция Right Agent.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/onsails" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@onsails</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/203ddfdd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>203ddfdd</a>
- Добавлен поставщик языковой модели OpenCode Go.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/tevenfeng" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@tevenfeng</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c29c76e3" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c29c76e3</a>

**Улучшения**

- Для поиска на разных языках добавлены настройка языка и токенизации BM25 и необязательный сервер PGroonga.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/cb04cb79" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>cb04cb79</a>
- Добавлена переменная среды для включения журнала доступа к API через Uvicorn.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/25387083" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>25387083</a>
- В Control Plane можно очистить ментальную модель через интерфейс.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/6e9b741b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>6e9b741b</a>
- Повышено качество консолидации наблюдений, в том числе с учётом времени.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d1ef9da9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d1ef9da9</a>
- Control Plane теперь поддерживает восемь языковых вариантов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/486c3a8b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>486c3a8b</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- После удаления устаревшие исходящие связи теперь пересчитываются асинхронно.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/cc3ba4a3" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>cc3ba4a3</a>
- Настройка Codex больше не берёт унаследованные базовые URL и автоматически обновляет токены доступа OAuth.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/795c081d" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>795c081d</a>
- Параметр temperature больше не отправляется в запросах Anthropic из-за несовместимости API.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/dabbf9ff" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>dabbf9ff</a>
- Устранена повторная пакетная доставка событий сохранения через вебхук.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xmh1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xmh1011</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/6348f424" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>6348f424</a>
- В пакетных запросах сохранения большие отдельные записи теперь делятся на части.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c3b2b154" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c3b2b154</a>
- Исправлена поддержка Ollama: добавлен поставщик Ollama Cloud и вход для облачных адресов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/cb037290" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>cb037290</a>
- Выключенные инструменты больше не попадают в системный запрос агента.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/2582b45a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>2582b45a</a>
- Если поставщик векторов возвращает неверную размерность, запрос больше не повторяется.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/ai-ag2026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@ai-ag2026</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/e1e1a5e0" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>e1e1a5e0</a>
- Фигурные скобки в полях запроса пользователя теперь экранируются, чтобы форматирование не сбоило.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/67ae2a41" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>67ae2a41</a>
- Исправлено обновление базовой ментальной модели и ограничен размер истории, чтобы JSON в базе не переполнялся.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xmh1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xmh1011</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/44b34c89" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>44b34c89</a>
- Добавлена обработка gzip, чтобы сжатые ответы графа можно было разобрать.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/31d1e172" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>31d1e172</a>
- Для каждой операции ограничено число параллельных вызовов модели, чтобы избежать перегрузки.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/daf2348b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>daf2348b</a>
- При обновлении тегов теперь верно сохраняются их группы и триггеры.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xmh1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xmh1011</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/9c161e4e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>9c161e4e</a>
- При переранжировании распознаются уже нормированные оценки, а при нужде применяется нормировка по рангу.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xuli500177" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xuli500177</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/dcf5588e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>dcf5588e</a>
- При поиске сущностей метки пользователя больше не сопоставляются неверно по нечёткому совпадению.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/46dd2dfd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>46dd2dfd</a>
- Рабочие процессы теперь лучше обрабатывают устаревшие ожидающие процедуры схемы.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/xmh1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@xmh1011</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/592f01bb" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>592f01bb</a>
- В Windows встроенный интерфейс теперь находит верный путь к npx до запуска.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/tuancookiez-hub" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@tuancookiez-hub</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/2e5186a6" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>2e5186a6</a>
- Control Plane теперь проверяет подпись cookie сеанса, а не лишь его наличие.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/878ef957" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>878ef957</a>

## [0.6.2](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.6.2)

**Новые возможности**

- В интеграции Claude Code добавлено настраиваемое время ожидания MCP, чтобы долгие вызовы recall и reflect не обрывались по стандартному лимиту.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/rsaulo" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@rsaulo</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/55ef7067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>55ef7067</a>

**Улучшения**

- В Agent SDK параметр `max_results` инструмента `agent_knowledge_recall` переименован в `max_tokens`; значение по умолчанию выросло с 10 до 1024 для полезного результата поиска.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/d9dd1499" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>d9dd1499</a>
- В плагине MCP для Claude Code параметр `max_results` также переименован в `max_tokens`, чтобы обе интеграции совпадали.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/offendingcommit" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@offendingcommit</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/909a4fd4" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>909a4fd4</a>

**Безопасность**

- Обновлены уязвимые зависимости по отчётам GitHub CVE: litellm до ≥1.83.14, urllib3 до 2.7.0 и другие пакеты pip и npm с серьёзными уязвимостями.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/dcbouius" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@dcbouius</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/fd05bdab" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>fd05bdab</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Параметр времени/Event Date команды “memory retain” теперь передаёт заданное время в API.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/51ea9aa2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>51ea9aa2</a>
- Исправлена миграция базы данных, затронувшая подтипы ментальной модели в текущей версии схемы.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/debbd919" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>debbd919</a>
- При миграции размерности векторов теперь обрабатываются временные ошибки OID базы данных.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/dcbouius" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@dcbouius</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/9dfbfb4b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>9dfbfb4b</a>
- Вызовы `get_page` в знаниях Claude Code теперь запрашивают `detail=content` и не смешивают результаты инструментов, поэтому полный текст страницы возвращается надёжно.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/cdbartholomew" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@cdbartholomew</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b2a693ab" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b2a693ab</a>
- Та же правка `detail=content` внесена в `agent_knowledge_get_page` Agent SDK, чтобы клиенты тоже получали полный текст страницы.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/r266-tech" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@r266-tech</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b593d40f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b593d40f</a>
- Интеграция Paperclip приведена к реальному формату событий, чтобы факты снова верно извлекались из входящих данных.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/amirhmoradi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@amirhmoradi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/be908d5b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>be908d5b</a>

## [0.6.1](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.6.1)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлена поддержка векторного индекса AlloyDB ScaNN для быстрого поиска в установках AlloyDB<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/can1357" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@can1357</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/e4422a9b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>e4422a9b</a>
- Добавлены метки сущностей типа map: при извлечении можно получать группы полей, например адрес, вместо плоской строки<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/2b725bc4" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>2b725bc4</a>
- Добавлен поставщик языковых моделей z.ai (智谱) с моделью по умолчанию, доступной на бесплатном уровне<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/Burgunthy" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@Burgunthy</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/4c75cd9e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>4c75cd9e</a>
- Добавлен поставщик litellmrouter: API может обращаться к нескольким адресам моделей с автоматической цепочкой резервных вариантов<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/98f33cbf" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>98f33cbf</a>
- Для поиска памяти добавлена необязательная база только для чтения: запросы идут на реплику, а запись остаётся на основной базе<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/cdbartholomew" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@cdbartholomew</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/cf9b1f59" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>cf9b1f59</a>
- Для защиты интерфейса Control Plane добавлен необязательный вход по ключу без полной настройки единого входа<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/ariel-ai-bot" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@ariel-ai-bot</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c0ff87ea" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c0ff87ea</a>
- В команде `memory retain-files` добавлен флаг `--strategy` для выбора способа деления файлов перед сохранением<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/375747f5" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>375747f5</a>
- В списке банков Control Plane теперь видно число записей памяти в каждом банке<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/5a0cb4a5" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>5a0cb4a5</a>

**Улучшения**

- Сбор статистики прогресса рабочих процессов теперь охватывает лишь схемы с ожидающими задачами, не опрашивая простаивающих арендаторов<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/39d31ad2" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>39d31ad2</a>

**Исправления ошибок**

- Убраны вложенные повторы, из-за которых один временный сбой модели вызывал много новых попыток извлечения фактов<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a22e8bdd" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a22e8bdd</a>
- Reflect теперь берёт метаданные документа из исходных параметров сохранения, чтобы ответ содержал верный контекст<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nepenth" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nepenth</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/f2a2f9fe" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>f2a2f9fe</a>
- При сбое batch_retain дочерняя ошибка error_message передаётся основной операции, чтобы вместо общей ошибки показывать её причину<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/cdbartholomew" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@cdbartholomew</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/7b82d05b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>7b82d05b</a>
- В фоновой службе macOS вызов os.fork() заменён на subprocess.Popen для совместимости с PyTorch MPS<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/8d77976a" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>8d77976a</a>
- Усилен вход в Control Plane по ключу; ошибки стали яснее, а переадресация работает лучше<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/b628716f" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>b628716f</a>
- После сохранения ссылки на текст очищаются, чтобы долгие рабочие процессы не исчерпывали память на больших партиях<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c9145805" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c9145805</a>
- В образе Docker для /home/hindsight заданы права 755, чтобы контейнер работал и с --user UID:GID<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a5cef602" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a5cef602</a>
- Мета-пакеты hindsight-api, hindsight-all и hindsight-all-slim жёстко привязаны к той же версии hindsight-api-slim, чтобы после обновления не оставалась старая slim-версия<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a86d5381" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a86d5381</a>
- Перед вызовом необязательной функции schemas_with_pending_work() рабочий процесс проверяет pg_proc, чтобы старые базы без неё не вызывали сбой<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/a1c1b7de" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>a1c1b7de</a>
- Клиент TypeScript теперь получает CLIENT_VERSION через tsup define, чтобы всегда сообщать верную версию выпущенного пакета<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/ab8cc3e6" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>ab8cc3e6</a>

## [0.6.0](https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/releases/tag/v0.6.0)

**Новые возможности**

- Добавлена интеграция Dify с инструментами памяти Hindsight в процессах Dify.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/bc23750b" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>bc23750b</a>
- Добавлен пакет узла сообщества n8n для работы с памятью Hindsight в автоматизации n8n.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c1eaf711" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c1eaf711</a>
- Добавлена интеграция SmolAgents с инструментами памяти Hindsight для агентов на её базе.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/8314de5e" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>8314de5e</a>
- Добавлена интеграция AWS Bedrock AgentCore Runtime (hindsight-agentcore).<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/benfrank241" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@benfrank241</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/c91696f5" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>c91696f5</a>
- Для корпоративного хранения добавлена поддержка базы Oracle.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/DK09876" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@DK09876</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/50f559c9" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>50f559c9</a>
- Долгие асинхронные операции теперь можно отменять.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/nicoloboschi" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@nicoloboschi</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/7f30dcc7" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>7f30dcc7</a>
- Клиент TypeScript теперь поддерживает AbortSignal во всех методах для отмены запросов.<span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/harryplusplus" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-primary)", textDecoration: "none", display: "inline-flex", alignItems: "center", gap: "4px", verticalAlign: "middle"}}>@harryplusplus</a><span style={{color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-500)", margin: "0 0.3em"}}>·</span><a href="https://github.com/vectorize-io/hindsight/commit/8367930c" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style={{fontFamily: "var(--ifm-font-family-monospace, monospace)", fontSize: "0.85em", color: "var(--ifm-color-emphasis-600)"}}>8367930c</a>

**Улучшения**
